Model before rewriting:

letting V be domain variant {a: bool, b: int(0, 4)}
find x: V
find y1: V
find y2: V
find z: V
find t1: V
find t2: V
find someInt: int(0)

such that

x[a] = true,
y1 = variant{a = false},
y2 = variant{b = 4},
x = z,
t1 = variant{b = someInt},
t2 = variant{b = t1[b]}

y1
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
y1
new variables:

   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])

   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])

  find someInt#sign_magnitude_2: bool
new constraints:
  and([!(someInt#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)])
  !(someInt#sign_magnitude_2)
  (!(someInt#sign_magnitude_2)) -> (and([!(someInt#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]))
  (someInt#sign_magnitude_2) -> (or([someInt#sign_magnitude_1;int(1..)]))
  or([SATInt(SignMagnitude, [someInt#sign_magnitude_1,someInt#sign_magnitude_2;int(1..)] [0, 0]) = 0;int(1..)])
